### Anuvaka XXIII

1\. Waters were, verily, this (creation), water only. That one Prajapati came forth in a lotus-petal. In the midst of his mind the Kama (Desire) was produced: May I create this (world)\! Therefore, what a man thinks in the mind, speaks that by the speech and performs that by action. As to that this is (sald):-

2\. Kama (Desire) came forth in the very beginning, which was the first (flow of) semen of the mind (1). The intelligent sages, deciding in their hearts, found the relation of the being (sat) in the non-being (aset).

3\. Who knows thus, to him that comes which he desires.

4\. He (Prajapati) performed austerity. After performing austerity he shook (his) body. Which was his flesh, from that stood the seers Arunas, Ketus and Vatarasanas (2). Which (were) the nails, they (became) Vaikhan as as; which (we re) the hairs, they became Valakhilyas; which (was) the elixir, that (became a tortoise in the midst) of the waters.

5\. (Prajapati) said to him, creeping in the (water) in the shape of a tortoise "(You) are born from my skin and flesh (3)". "No" he said, "I was here before (you)". That is (Purvam as am) the (etimological essence) Purusstva of the Purusa (person). He stood being thousand-headed (and) thousand-eyed. (Prajapati) said to him "You have been before (me), (so) do this (creation) as (my) predecessor"

In this Anuvaks the praise of the waters and the Aruneke tuka Fire are given by giving the description of the creation and quoting some Res.

7\. Now Aruna Ketu (personified by that Puruse in the shape of the tortoise) put (the water) southward (uttering the Mantra) "O Agni, you are the obtainable things". Then stood forth the fire (Agni); that (in) the southern quarter. Now Aruna Ketu put (the water) westward (uttering the Mantra) "O Vayu, you are the obtainable things (5)." Then stood forth the wind (Vayu); that (is) the western quarter. Now Aruna Ketu put (the water) northward uttering the Mantra) "O Indra, you are the obtainable things". Then stood forth Indra; that (1s) the northern quarter. Now, Aruna Ketu put (the water) in the middle (uttering the Mantra) "O Pusan, you are the obtainable things". Then stood forth Pusan; that is this (belowward) quarter(6). Now, Aruna Ketu put (the water) above (uttering the Mantra) "o goda, you are the obtainable things". Then stood forth the gods, the men, the Gandharvas and the Apsarases; that is the aboveward quarter.

8\. Which drops fell hither and thither, by them stood forth the Asuras, the Raksases and the Pisacas; therefore, they were defeated, for they were born of the drops.

11\. (For the creation was from the loose waters,) therefore, all this became as loose and not fixed.

12\. Prajapati, indeed, making himself the soul entered that very (creation).

### Anuvaka V

1\. May this fire put your life all round; your breath

(Prana) comes again; I destroy your disease away.

2\. 0 Fire, become the giver of the life taking oblations, beginning with ghee and caused by ghee; drinking the sweet and clean ghee of the cow protect this (secrificer), as a father (protects) the son.

3\. 0 Fire, make this (sacrificer) powerful for life and brilliance; 0 Varuna,make the sharp vitality more sharp; o aditi, give him comfort as a mother (gives to her son), O All-gods (Visvedevas), make him so that he may be alive till the old age.

4\. 0 Fire, make our lives pure; impell vigour and food

(to come) to us; destroy sway the poverty.

5\. (One) become consecrated in a year; he purifies himself by the year.

6\. (One) becomes consecrated in a month; what is a month, that is the year (because the year is the collection of the months); (hence he) purifies himself by the year. (One) becomes consecrated in twenty-four nights; for there are twenty-four half months in a year; (hence he) purifies himself by the year. (One) becomes consecrated in twelve nights; for there are twelve months in a year; (hence he) purifies himself by the year. (One) becomes consecrated in six nights; for there are six seasons in a year, (hence he) purifies himself by the year. (One) becomes consecrated in three nights; for there are three feet in the Gayatri (Mantra); (hence he) purifies himself by the Gayatri (Mantra).

1\. Brahma, (who is) born himself, went (abhyanareat) to the white performers of the austerity, (who were) not born (11ke ourselves). ### Anuvaka X

1\. These five great sacrifices are spread always, finished the sacrifice of the gods, the sacrifice of the fathers, the sacrifice of the creatures, the sacrifice of the men, the sacrifice of the Brahman. Thus.

2\. What, even a fuel, he puts in the fire that is finished the sacrifice of the gods.

3\. What he makes the Svadha (hail) even by the waters, that is finished the sacrifice of the fathers.

4\. What he takes as oblation for the creatures, that is finished the sacrifice of the creatures.

5\. What food he gives to the Brahmanas, that is finished the sacrifice of the men.

6\. Whatever he reads either a Rc or a Yajus or a Saman, that is finished the sacrifice of the Brahmen.

7\. When he reads the Rces, the streams of the milk carry Svadha (hall) for the fathers; when he reads the Yajuses, the streams of the ghee (carry Svadha for the fathers); when he reads the Samans, the Soma-juice flows for them; when he reads the Atharvangiras (-Mantras), the streams of the honey (flows for them); when he reads the Brahmanas, Itihas as (histories), Puranas, Kalpas, Gathas, Naras amsins (the praises of the heroes), the stream of the fat carry the Svadha (hall) for the fathers.

(he should go) out of the village in the east, or in the midquarter between the east and the north or in the north so far that the roofs etc. of the village become not observable. ### Anuvaka XII

1\. Hund red-headed Purusa (Person) having hundred eyes, hundred feet, after covering all the worlds stood larger (than these) by ten fingers.

2\. That all is Purusa (only), whatever was (before) or what \-ever will be (further); He is the Lord of the immortality (gods) as well as of those, who are born (living on) by the food.

3\. This is His greatness; Purusa (Person) Himself is greater than this (1); all the beings are His (one) foot; His other three feet are in the heaven (in the form of) nectar.

4\. Purus a of three feet rises above; His feet is also here again; then He pervaded various ones who eat or who do not eat (as mountains etc.).

5\. of Him Virat was born; of Virat a person was born; after being born he became (various) other than Virat; after him the earth and the bodies were born (2).

6\. When the gods performed the sacrifice by the oblation of the person, then Vasant (spring season) was its ghee, Grisma (summer) was fuel and Sarad (autumn) was the oblation.

The Purusa Sukta. (From 1 to 18).

7\. Seven were the Paridhis (enclosures) of this and twentyone were the fuels, when the gods bound the person as cattle (for sacrificing).

8\. They sprinkled in Barhi-gress (by mind) that born before (the other beings) 13); by him the gods, the sages and the seers sacrificed.

9\. From that sacrifice, where all (in the form of the person) was sacrificed came forth the wonderful ghee and that produced those cattle who are related to the wind, who are wild and who are of the village (tame).

10\. From that sacrifice, where all (in the form of the person) was sacrificed, came forth the Rees, the Samans, the verses came forth from that, the Yajus was produced from that (4).

11\. From that the horses were produced and also those, who have teeth in the sides of the mouth (as ses etc.) were born; the cows came forth from that; the goats and the sheep were born of him.

12\. In how many ways they caused the person, when they caused the person? What is called his mouth? What are the hands? What are the thighs? And what are called the legs?

13\. Brahmana (caste) was his mouth; Ksatriya was his hands 15); Vaisya was his thighs and Sudra was born of the legs. 14\. The moon was born of (his) mind; the sun was born of the eye, Indra and the fire were born of the mouth and the wind was born of the breath.

15\. From the navel the mid-region was born; from the head moved round the heaven; from the legs the earth and from the ear the quarters came forth; in that way they caused all the world 16).

### Anuvaka XIII

1\. He was born of the waters and the elixirs of the earth; he moved round in the rule of Visvakarman (causer of the world); Tvastr makes the forms of him; I know that (greatness) of the Purusa (person) first.

2\. I know this great Puruse of the colour of the sun, out of the darkness; he, who knows him thus, becomes immortal here; there is no other way for reaching the (immortality).

## Prapathaka IV

[[P175]]

ANUVAKA 1

146

Harih Om.

1\. Salutation to the speech, which has been uttered, salutation to that speech which is yet unuttered; salutation to the speech, salutation to the lord of the speech (Vacaspsti); salutation to the seers, the makers of the Mantras, the lords of the Mantras; may not the seers, the makers of the Mantras, the lords of the Mantres disobey me; may I not disobey the seers, the makers of the Mantras, the lords of the Mantras.

### Anuvaka II

1\. One sacrifices the oblation for Savitr for impelling.

5\. Then the definition of the sacrifice is left; (there- fore, who should utter only Yajus-Mentre ; by this he does not put the sacrificer in the fire as the oblation, nor the definitions of the sacrifice is left.

6\. The Osystri thought the other metres crossed by it; the Vasat-making (god), reaching there cut the head of it; from that came forth the juice of two kinds; the half of that entered the earth and the other half entered the cattle. Which entered the earth 131, that became the hadirs tree (Acacia catechu); which entered the cattle, that became the goat. Which is the Abhri (digging instrument), made of the Thedire, that fulfills the head of the sacrifice, indeed, by the juice of the metres.

7\. which is of the Udumbara (Ficus Glomerata)

\---- vigour, verily, is the Udumbara that fulfills the head of the sacrifice, indeed, by the vigour which is of the Venu (bamboo) \- brilliance, verily, is the Venu (4) by the brilliance, indeed, that fulfills the head of the sacrifice. Which is of the Vikankat a tree, by that he obtains the shining.

8\. He takes the Abhri (digging instrument) (by the Mantra:) "by the impelling of the god savitr", for the sake of impelling; he says, "by the arms of Asvins", for Asvins are the Adhvaryu-priests of the gods; he says, "by the hands of Pusan", for going (-capacity).

9\. This is like the thunderbolt, which is this Abhri; he says, "you are Abhri, you are woman" for the peace (5).

10\. He says, "for the gods, the makers of the Adhvara"; the sacrifice is, verily, the Adhvers, hence he says, verily, "for the gods, the makers of the sacrifice."

[[P217]]

187

11\. He says, "O Brahmanaspati, stand up"; he goes to obtain the head of the sacrifice by Brahman, indeed.

12\. He says, "may Brahmanaspati come well"; he obtains the head of the sacrifice going first, indeed.

13\. He says, "may the goddess speech come"; for the sacrifice is, verily, the speech.

14\. He says, "for obtaining the manly Mahavira pot, the cooker of the five foods" (6); for the sacrifice is fivefold.

15\. He says, "may the gods carry our sacrifice"; he makes the gods the leaders of the sacrifice.

16\. He says, "may the goddesses heaven and earth assent me"; he fulfills the head of the sacrifice being assented by these. 17\. He says, "may I prosper today, this is the head of the Makha"; the sacrifice is, verily, the Makha; hence he says, verily, "may I propper today, this is the head of the sacrifice." 18\. He says, "I take you for the sacrifice, I put you on the head of the sacrifice"; he takes that decidedly 17).

19\. He takes thrice; three are these worlds; he fulfills He takes the head of the sacrifice for these worlds, verily. silently at the fourth time; he fulfills the head of the sacrifice by the innumerable (objects).

20\. He takes further from the pit; therefore, the pit is most merciful

21\. He says, "you were this much before"; he fulfills the head of the sacrifice fully in this earth, indeed. is, verily, the vigour, 22\. What is this ant-hill, that which the white-ants take out (8); which is the preparation of the fat of the white-ants; by that he obtains the vigour, the juice of the earth; and also the ear; for this is the ear of the earth, which is the ant-hill.

[[P218]]

188

23\. He becomes not de af, who knows thus.

24\. Indra raised the thunderbolt for Vrtra; wherever he showed bravery 191, therever he (Vrtza) was not caught; he made effort near the Putikas tamba (Caesalpinia Bonducella); he (Vrtra) was caught. He (Indra) said, "you kept my protection"; that is the Utikatva (etimological essence) of the Utikas (Putikas). What are the Utikas (Putika trees) they keep the protection of the sacrifice.

25\. He says, "you are born of the fire, the semen of Prajapati"; he obtains that juice, verily, which entered the cattle (100.

26\. Five are the preparations; for the sacrifice is, verily, fivefold; he fulfills the head of that, whatever is this sacrifice.

27\. If he prepares by the skin of the village-cattle, he troubles the village-cattle, he troubles the village-cattle; he prepares by the skin of the black antelope; he troubles, verily, the wild animals; therefore, among the born animals of the same time 11 the wild ones are less; for they have been troubled.

28\. He fulfills in the place of the hairs, hence this

(skin of black antelope) is pure for the sacrifice.

29\. They come holding for destroying the Rakseses (demons). Many men bring, by that they put honour in that. They put that on the sandy and encircled place for the sake of the peace.

30\. He mixes with the hot waters (12); 1.e., he puts brilliance in that.

[[P219]]

189

31\. He says, "may the honey make you sweet"; 1.e., he puts brilliance in that by Brahman.

32\. If he touches by the village-pots, then he troubles the village-pots; he makes that with the Armakapala (old earth-vessels of the destroyed villages); these are not worth earning, so he troubles only these.

33\. He makes that with the sugars for the firmness and for the propitiation.

34\. He makes that with the hairs of the goat; this is the dear body of the fire; he makes that with its dear body, indeed, and also with the brilliance.

35\. He makes that with the hairs of the skin of the black antelope; the sacrifice is, verily, the skin of the black antelope; he makes the sacrifice, indeed, with the sacrifice.

### Anuvaka III

1\. He makes in an encircled place for holding the brilliance of the sacred knowledge.

2\. Making that he should not breathe; if he breathes troubles the Pranas (breaths); he breathes while making that, he moving in the side for the protection of his breaths.

3\. One should not go between the Pravargya and the sun; if he goes between them, he may become of bad skin 11; therefore, one should not be between them, for his own protection.

\* Preparation of the Kshevire-pot has been described in this Anuvaka with the details.

[[P220]]

190

4\. One should go by the bamboo; the bamboo is, verily, the brilliance; the Pravargye is (also) the brilliance; he increases the brilliance by the brilliance.

5\. He says, "you are the head of the Makha"; the sacrifice is, verily, the Melche; this is the head of that, which ia this Pravargya 121; so he says thus.

6\. He says, "you are the feet of the sacrifice"; for these are the feet of the sacrifice; and (he says this) for the firm-standing.

7\. He says, "I perform you by the Gayatri-verse"; he performs that by the verses.

8\. He performs that by thrice giving; three are, verily, these worlds; for obtaining these worlds.

9\. He performs by the verses 131; the verses are, verily, the vigour; he performs that by the vigour.

Prajapati.

10\. He makes the hole by the Yajus-Mantra for the opening. 11\. He makes this much like the sacrificial mouth of

12\. He makes this much like the articles of the sacrifice. 13\. He makes this much; this much is the vigour in the person; like the power (he makes) (4). measurable.

14\. He makes non-measurable for obtaining the non-

15\. He makes round (like the neck) for holding.

16\. He says, "be heated by the heat of the sun"; this is according to the Yajus-Mantra.

17\. He heats by the excrements of the horse; the horse is, verily, related to Prajapati; (he does so) for equal cause.

[[P221]]

191

He says, "you are the issue of the sprinkling horse"; that sun is, verily, the sprinkling horse; of him metres are the issues (5); he heats that by the metres.

19\. He says, "for the shining, for the burning"; he puts the shining in it.

20\. for the peace.

He becomes shined by Varuna and gone with Mitra

21\. He says, "I take you for the performance"; this is according to the Yajus-Mantre.

22\.

He says, "may the god Savitr take you above"; he puts that above, which is caused by savitr, with Brahman and with the gods.

23\. He says, "fulfill the quarters being not contained on the earth" 160; therefore, the fire shines in all the quarters. 24\. He says, "stand up, be increased and stand firm above" for the firm-standing.

25\. He is sufficient to become blind, who sees the Pravargya; so he says, "I see you by the eye of the sun" for the protection of the eye.

26\. He says, "I see you for the straight, good and well-residing (places)"; this earth is straight; the mid-region is good and that heaven is well-residing (7); the quarters are the prosperity; he makes these worlds for him; and also (he does so) for the firm-standing.

27\. He says, "This I make firm that the son of that with the subject, cattle and brilliance of the sacred knowledge"; he makes firm with the subject, cattle and brilliance of the sacred knowledge.

[[P222]]

192

28\. He should say, "with the subject" for the ruling caste; he makes him firm with the subject, indeed; he should say, "with cattle "for the Vaisya caste; he makes him firm with the cattle, indeed.

29\. The pot of the Asuras is not sprinkled (8); he makes that sprinkled for the gods.

30\. He sprinkles with the milk of the goat; this is the great milk, which is the milk of the goat; he sprinkles that by the great milk.

31\. By the Yajus-Mantre for the peace.

32\. He sprinkles with the metres; this is, verily, done by the metres; he sprinkles the metres with the metres.

33\. He says, "sprinkle the speech", he obtains the speech; he says, "sprinkle the vigour", he obtains the vigour; he says, "sprinkle the oblation", he makes the oblation.

34\. He says, "O god of infront, I hang you above"; this is according to the Yajus-Mentre.

### Anuvaka IV

1\. He says, "0 Brahman, we perform the Pravargye;

Hotr (priest), praise the Gharma"; who is the Hotr-priest, he is, verily, now Brhaspati; he performs the Pravergys addressing in this way for not being troubled himself.

2\. He says, "I sprinkle you for Yama and for Makha"; these are the gods of this; he increases this by these (gods), indeed.

\* Use of the Mantras for placing the Mahavira. (From 1 to 43).

[[P223]]

193 3\. He sprinkles by the hot waters; (by this) he puts the brilliance in him (in the sacrificer) 11).

4\. He sprinkles respectively; hence he puts brilliance in him respectively.

5\. He sprinkles thrice; for the sacrifice is threefold (having three Savanas); and (he does so) for the purity.

Raksases.

6.The Hotr-priest repeats the saying for the killing of the

7\. He (does so) without taking the breath for the continuation of the life of the sacrificer.

8\. He repeats the metres as the Gayatri (though) being (really) Tristups (2); for the Prana-breath is related to the Gayatri-metre; hence he puts Prana, indeed, in the sacrificer. 9\. He repeats (the metres) linked together; for the continuation of the Pranas (breaths) and the food etc. and also forkilling the Rakseres.

10\. If he repeats the measured (metres), then he obtains the measured (objects); hence he repeats non-measured (metres) for obtaining non-measured (objects).

11\. This is the head of the sacrifice 131, which is the Pravergys; the Munje-grasses are the vigour, which is the Veda of the Munja-grasses; by that one increases the head of the sacrifice as by the vigour.

12\. He sacrifices the oblations for the breaths; hence he puts the Prenes in the sacrificer.

13\. He sacrifices seven oblations; seven are, verily, the breaths of the head; hence he puts the breaths in the sacrificer.

14\. He says, "may the god Savitr mix you with the honey" 140; he mixes him with the brilliance.

[[P224]]

194

15\. By "protect the earth from the heat" he puts the gold for not making the earth much heated.

16\. Which is the Pravargy a-sacrifice, that is the head of the sacrifice; the fire is the all gods; burning the scattered Munje-grasses he puts (them beneath the Mahavira-pot); hence he puts the head of the sacrifice, vetily, among the gods.

17\. The fore-parts of the Munje-grasses (should not) be scattered; for they are in the place of the Darbha-grasses here. 18\. He says, "you are the ray, you are the shining"; he puts in him the splendour and the brilliance of the sacred knowledge.

19\. He says, "sit well, you are great", for it is great. 20\. The speakers of the sacred knowledge say that same are the priests as in bhe Darsapurnamasa-sacrifices, then why the Hotr-priest does not utter benedictions for the sacrificer. (In reply they find that) some sacrifices have the benedictions in the beginning and some have the benedictions in the following parts. 21\. What Yajus-antras he says, "not injurable in the east"; (by those) the sacrificer obtains the benedictions, indeed, from the head (hhe Pravargya).

22\. He mentioned life in the east, offspring in the south, breath in the west, ear in the north, holding above; (by these) he puts the breaths in his favour.

23\. He is able to be mad, whom they move in the quarters 171; hence by "you are the pervading of Manu (men), having many sons" he touches the earth; this earth is, verily, the pervading of Manu (men), having many sons; he stands firm in this earth not to be mad.

[[P225]]

195

24\. He says, "be well enjoyable for me; do not, do not kill" for non-violance.

25\. He says, "you are collected, you are collected well";

(by this) he puts the worship, indeed, in him.

26\. Which is the Pravargya-sacrifice, that is the head of the sacrifice. That sun is, verily, the Pravargya; Maruts are the rays of that 18). He says, "Hail for Maruts, be spread all over"; (by this) he makes the sun pervade all over with the rays. Therefore, that is pervading in that world with the rays. Therefore, the king is pervading with the subject. Therefore, the chief of the village is pervading with the co-born ones.

27\. For, the shining of the burnt fire reached to the Vikenketa tree; so, what are the encirclings of the Vikankata tree, (by those) he obtains the shining.

28\. These (encirclings) are twelve (9); for there are twelve months in a year; he obtains, indeed, the year.

29\. Some say that there is a thirteenth month; which is the thirteenth encirclings, by that he obtains the thirteenth months. 30\. He says, "you are the inner holder of the mid- region" for the discrimination.

31\. saying, "protect the he even from the heat" he puts the gold above for not making that heaven much heated.

32\. He says, "O able one, you hold the bow and the arrows" (101; (by this) he praises this.

33\. saying, "you are related to the Gayatri, Tristup and Jegati metres" he takes the fan; he takes these by the metres.

34\. He fans saying, "honey, honey"; Prana (breath) is, verily, the honey; he puts the Prana in the sacrificer.

[[P226]]

196

35\. They move thrice round that; for the Prane (breath) is threefold.

36\. They move thrice round that; for the sacrifice is threefold (11); and also (they do so) for the killing of the Reksanes (demona).

37\. They move thrice again round that; six movings become performed; six are, verily, the sess one; they stand firmly in the seasons.

38\. He, who attacks the dear body of the Gharma, becomes of bad skin. He attacks the dear body of this who, after making three rounds makes the fourth round. Rajani, the lord of the cruel (priests) attacked this (in this way) 112); then he became of bad skin; therefore, after the third round one should not make the fourth one for his protection.

39\. Pranas (breaths) are, verily, the fans.

40\. They fan separately for not making the non-seperation of the Pranas (breaths).

41\. They fan sitting variously; hence they atend firm in the quarters.

42\. They fen above for obtaining well the heavenly world. 43\. They fan all round; hence this wind blows all round.

### Anuvaka V

1\. He says, "may the fire, with Vasus, make you shine in the east by the Gayatri metre"; the fire, indeed, makes him shine in the east with Vasus by the Gayatri metre.

Use of the Mantras of the Prapathaka IV.

[[P227]]

197

2\. He says, "may Indra, with Rudras, make you shine in the south by the Tristup-metre"; Indra, indeed, with Rudras, make him shine by the Tristup metre. He says, "may you make me shine being shined"; he asks the benediction in this way. He says, "may Varuna, with Adityas, make you shine in the west by the Jagatimetre"; Varuna, indeed, with Adity as makes him shine in the west by the Jagati-metre (1). He says, "may you make me shine being shined"; he asks the benediction in this way. He says, "may the heavenly Maruta, with Maruts, make you shine in the north by the Anustup-metre", the heavenly Meruts, with Maruts, indeed, makes him shine in the north by the Anustup-metre. He says, "may you make me shine being shined"; he asks the benediction in this way. He says, "may Brhaspati, with all-goda (Visvedevas), make you shine above by the Pankti-metre". Brhaspati, with all-gods (Visvedevas), indeed, makes him shine above by the Pankti-metre. He says, "may you make me shine being shined"; he asks benediction in this way (2). 3\. He says, "O god, Gharma, you are shining among the gods"; for this is shining in the gods. He says, "may I be shining among the men"; he, indeed, shines among the men.

4\. He says, "you are among the gods emperor, shining, full of life, brilliant and having the splendour of the sacred knowledge"; for this is shining, full of life, brilliant and having the splendour of the sacred knowledge among the gods. says, "may I become shining, full of life, brilliant and having the splendour of the secred knowledge among the men"; he becomes, indeed, shining, full of life, brilliant and having the splendour of the sacred knowledge among the men.

[[P228]]

198

5\. He says, "you are the shining, put the shining in me,

(yea) the shining in me"; (by this) he asks this benediction.

6\. If he says, "O Gharma, you are shining"without making him shine by these Yajus-Mantras, then the Adhvaryu becomes not shining and also the sacrificer becomes not shining. If he says, "O Gharma, you are shining" making him shine by these Yajus aMantras; then the Adhvaryu-priest becomes ahining, and also the sacrificer becomes shining (3).

### Anuvaka VI

1\. Which is this Pravargya, that is the head of the sacrifice, the neck are the Upas ad (-sacrifices); he begins (puts milk in the hot ghee) the Pravargys before the Up as ad s; hence he puts the he ad on the necks.

2\. He puts the milk in the hot ghee thrice; three are these worlds; he obtains the head of the sacrifice from these worlds. 3\. Six become performed; six are, verily, the seasons(1); he obtains the head of the sacrifice from the seasons.

4\. He puts the ghee in the hot milk twelve times; twelve are the months in a year; he obtains the head of the sacrifice from the year.

5\. Twenty-four become performed; twenty-four are the half months in a year; he obtains the head of the sacrifice from the half-months.

6\. And some say that only once should be poured the milk in the hot ghee; for one is the he ad (20.

[[P229]]

199

7\. He performs the Pravargya (pours) the milk in the hot ghee) in the Agniatoma-sacrifice. Whatever is the Agnistoma, that is the sacrifice; hence he puts the head of that whatever is the sacrifice.

8\. One should not perform the Pravargya in the Ukthya; progeny and cattle are the Ukthyes; if he performs the Pravargya in the Ukthya, he burns the progeny and cattle of the sacrificer. 9\. He performs the Pravargya in Visvajit and Sarvapratha sacrifices 13); the Prsthas destroy the non-destroyed (sine); by the Prsthas, verily, he obtains the fruit of the Pravargya for him destroying the non-destroyed (sins).

10\. He says, "I have seen the protector"; the Prana is, verily, the protector; he spreads the Prans in the progeny.

11\. He says, "I have seen the protector"; that is, verily, the protector; he protects these subjects; he makes that sun protector of the subjects. destroyed.

12\. He says, "non-destroyable" (4); for that is not

13\. He says, "moving in the ways infront and backward", for this (sun) moves infpont and backward.

14\. He says, "that (protects the subjects), gathered or gone in different places"; for this (sun) protects the subjects, gathered or gone in different places.

15\. He says, "that comes again and again in the worlds"; for this (sun) comes again and again in the worlds.

16\. He says, "you protected here by Madhu and Madhvi;

Madhu and Madhuci"; (by this) he makes the two months of the spring for him.

[[P230]]

200

17\. He says, "the fire has gone with the fire" (5);

(by this) he makes the two months of the summer season for him. 18\. He says, "the fire has gone with the fire"; for this fire (Mahavira) goes with the fire (sun).

19\. He says, "Hail\! the fire has gone with the heat"; it has been said before (about this), by this next he praises. 20\. He says, "you shine as the holder of the heaven, the region, the holder of the earth"; (by this) he makes the two months of the rainy season for him.

21\. He says, "(I see you) for the heart, for the mind", (by this) he makes the two months of the autumn for him 161\.

22\. He says, "give the sacrificings among the gods in the heaven"; by the sacrificings, verily, he holds together these worlds.

23\. He says, "O lord of all the earths"; (by this) he makes the two months of the dewy season for him.

24\. He says, "O Gharma, you are famous in the gods; protect the gods"; (by this) he makes the two months of the winter season for him.

25\. He says, "grant us the speech, which is born of the austerity, "going to the gods"; which is the pure speech, that is born of the austerity; he obtains laat, indeed (7).

26\. He says, "the womb of the gods"; for this is the womb of the gods.

27\. He says, "the father of the men"; the subjects are the men; which is the Pravargya, that is the father of them; therefore, he says thus.

28\. He says, "the lord of the subjects"; for this is the lord of the subjects.

[[P231]]

201

29\. He says, "the wisdom of the poets" 18); for this is the wisdom of the subjects.

30\. He says, "this god has done effort with the god Savitr and shined with the sun"; he praises that sun and this

Pravargye.

31\. He says, "o charms, you are the giver of life and brilliance to us"; he asks this benediction.

32\. He says, "you are our father; impell us as a father"; it impells him, indeed.

33\. Nine are, verily, these Avakas ss (Mantras); the tenth is for the wife; nine are, verily, the Pranas (breaths); the navel is the tenth; he puts the breaths in the sacrificer; and the Virat-Mantra is of ten syllables; the food is the Virat; he obtains the food etc. by the Virat, indeed.

34\. The head of the sacrifice was cut; the gods put that by the sacrificings; the priest see; this is, verily, the sacrificings; by the sacrificings, verily, he puts the head of the sacrifice (10).

35\. They see shiningly; for Prajapati created the offsprings from the shining; (he does so) for the creation of the offsprings.

36\. They see shiningly; for Parjanya (rain god) rains by the shining; (by doing so) Parjanya becomes desirous of raining and the offsprings increase.

37\. They see shiningly, for the shining is the brilliance of the sacred knowledge; they become full of the brilliance of the sacred knowledge (11). life.

38\. They see readingly; they go to the whole span of the

[[P232]]

202

39\. The wife should not see; if she sees, she gives birth and (the Mahavira) burns that born one; if she does not see, she does and not give birth and (the Mahavira) does not kill the born one; hence he says the Yajus-Mantras covering her (by anything); she gives birth and (the Mahavira) does not kill the born one of her.

40\. One says, "may I make sexual intercourse full of the form-making god"; by the intercourse, verily, the offsprings are born.

### Anuvaka VII

1\. Saying, "by the impelling of the god savitr I take you" he takes the girdle for the impelling. He says, "by the arma of Asvinas; for Asvine are the Adhvaryu-priests of the gods. saya, "by the hands of Pus an" for the going (-capacity).

2\. He says, "I take you, you are the girdle of Aditi" for making the use of the Yajus-Mantras.

3\. He says, "O Ida, come; 0 Aditi, come; 0 Sarasvati, come"; these are, verily, her divine names; by the divine names he calls her.

4\. He says, "o that.....come; 0 that.....come; O that.... come;" these are the names of this given by the men (1); he calls her by the names given by the men.

5\. Six are performed; six are, verily, the seasons; he calls her by the seasons.

6\. He says, "you are the turban for Aditi"; this is according to the Yajus-Mantra.

[[P233]]

203

7\. He says, "you, the son of Ida, are the wind"; for the calf is related to the god wind.

8\. He says, "May Pusen release you"; for the cattle are related to the god Pusan 12); he makes them released by their own god.

9\. He says, "for Asvines, cause to swell milk"; Asvinas are, verily, the physicians of the gods; he makes them, indeed, physician for him.

10\. He says, "which is your sleeping udder", (by this) he praises that (cow).

11\. He says, "o calf, leave the Cherme, protect the Charma, leave for the Gharma"; this is similar to the saying, "give to him".

12\. He says, "May Brhaspati go to you 131; Brhaspati is, verily, Brahman of the gods; he goes to her (cow) with Brahman, indeed.

13\. He says, "you are donators, you are worth sucking";

(by this) he makes them sacrificeable.

14\. He says, "separated by the blood" for the discrimination.

15\. He says, "cause to swell milk for Asvinas, cause to swell milk for Sarasvati, cause to swell milk for Pusan; cause to swell milk for Brhaspati"; he causes, verily, to swell these gods. 16\. He says, "cause to swell for Indra, cause to swell for Indra"; (by this) he increases Indra by his share.

17\. He says twice for Indra (4); hence Indra is the great share-holder among the gods.

[[P234]]

204

18\. saying, "you are of Gayatri-metre, of Tristup-metre, of Jagati-metre" he takes the Sapha and Upayama woods; he takes these, indeed, by the metres.

19\. He says, "come to me with the part of the vigour"; he makes, indeed, his share of the vigour.

20\. Asvinas had said, verily, while putting the head of the sacrifice, "the Vas at (Hail) should be made for us first"; hence he says, "o Indra and Asvinas, drink this sweet honey"; he makes, indeed, Vas at (Hail) for Asvin as first and increases

Asvinas by the share (5).

21\. He says, "o Vasus, sacrifice, Vat (Heil\!)"; he increases Vasus by the share.

22\. If he makes Vasat (Hail), his Vas at may become without essence; if he does not make Vas at, then the Raks ases may destroy the sacrifice; so he says Vat; 1.e., he makes Vas at imperceptibly; hence his vasat does not become without essence and nor the Raks ases destroy the sacrifice (6).

23\. He says, "Hall \! I sacrifice you for the ray of the sun that causes the rain"; which is his phous ray, that is the causer of the rain; he sacrifices this for that.

24\. He says, "you are the sweet oblation", (by this) he makes that sweet.

25\. He says, "heat even the heat of the sun"; this is according to the Yejus-Mantre.

26\. He says, "I take by the heaven and earth"; he takes that, indeed, by the heaven and the earth (71.

27\. He says, "I raise you by the mid-region"; he raises, indeed, by the mid-region; no man is able to hold that.

[[P235]]

205

28\. He says, "may I become able to hold you being assented by the gods and the fathers"; he holds, indeed, being assented by the gods and the fathers.

29\. He makes that improsperous who performs the Pravargya in the west and offers the oblations in the east; hence he says, "you are brilliance, follow the brilliance"; he puts brilliance in this.

30\. He says, for non-violence, "O touching the heaven do not kill that; touching the mid-region do not kill that; touching the earth do not kill that" (8).

31\. He says, "you are the paradise, grant me the paradise, grant me the heaven, protect me from the heaven"; (by this) he asks the benediction.

## Prapathaka VII

[[P273]]

THE TAITTIRIYA

240

ARANYAKA

Prapathaka VII

(The Taittiriya Upanisad, Siksa-valli)

First Anuvaka (Prayer for Auspices and Peace) Om Auspicious for us (may Mitra (be), auspicious (may) Varuna (be); auspicious for us may Aryaman be; auspicious for us (may) Indra (and) Brhaspati (be); auspicious for us (may) wide-strider Visnu (be).

Salutation to Brahman, salutation to you

O Vayu; you, indeed, are the perceptible Brahman; I will declare you, indeed, the perceptible Brahman; I will declare (you) settled order (rta), I will declare (you) truth. May That protect me, may That protect the speaker; may (That) protect may(that) protect the speaker.

Om Peace Peace Peace (1)

Second Anuvaka (Lesson on Orthoepy)

Om We will explain orthoepy: sound(varna), accent (avara), quantity (matra), force (bala), modulation (sama), combination( santana). Thus has been declared the lesson on orthoepy. (1)

Third Anuvaka (Prayer and mysticism of five combinations).

(May) glory (be) with us two'; (may) splendour of sacred knowledge (brahma-varcas) (be) with us two.

\* Prapathaka V according to Commentator Bhatt Bhaskar. He gives all the three Prapathakas VII, VIII & IX in one; hence the numbers of Anuvakas are continuous in his commentory.

1\. Teacher and pupil.

[[P274]]

Now next, we will explain the mystic meaning (upanisad) of combination (samhita) under five

241

headings : luminaries, concerning the worlds, concerning knowledge, concerning the concerning progeny (and) concerning the self. These are the great combinations, they say.

Now, concerning the worlds: earth is the prior form, heaven, the latter form, space, the conjunction(1), wind the connection; this much concerning the worlds. Now, concerning the luminaries: fire (is) the prior form, sun, the latter form, water, the conjunction, lightning, the connection. concerning the luminaries.

This much

Now, concerning knowledge: teacher (18) the prior form (2), pupil, the latter form, knowledge, the conjunction, instruction, the connection. much concerning knowledge.

This

Now, concerning progeny: mother is the prior form, father, the latter form, progeny, the conjunction, procreation, the connection. This much concerning progeny. (3)

Now, concerning the self: lower jaw is the prior form, upper jaw, the latter form, speech, the conjunction, tongue, the connection. concerning the self.

Thus these are the great combinations.

This much

He who knows these great combinations thus explained, is conjoined with progeny, cattle, splendour of sacred knowledge (brahma-varcas), eatable food and heavenly world. (4)

[[P275]]

Seventh Anuvaka (Brahman as fivefold)

Earth, mid-region, heaven, quarters, intermediate quarters; Fire(Agni), Wind(Vayu), Sun(Aditya), Moon (Candramas), Stars(Naksatras); waters, plants, trees, space(Akasa) and self (Atman): this much concerning material existance(adhibhuta).

Now concerning the self(adhyatma): Prana, vyana, Apana, Udana, Samana; eye, ear, mind (manas), speech, touch; skin, flesh, muscle, bone, marrow.

Having ordained this the seer said: 'Five-fold (Pankta), verily, is this all; with the fivefold, indeed, one strengthens the fivefold."(1)

### Anuvaka X

1\. The Self (Atman), less than an atom, greater that the great has been put in the cave (heart) of this being, by the delight of the disposer one, who is sorrowless, sees that lord, the greatness, which has no desires.

2\. Seven breath are born of him; seven rays, fuels and seven tongues (seven flames of the fire), these seven worlds in which the beings move all these sevenfold ones are established by that soul, put in the cave (heart).

3\. All the seas and the mountains are born of him; various formed rivers flow from that; from him came forth all the plants and the elixirs, connected with which this inner soul lives.

4\. Thet Atman (Self) is Brahman among the gods, knowledge (of words) among the poets, the seer among the learned ones (Vipras); buffalo among the animals, falcom among the vultures, cutting instrument among he woods; that (Atman) as Soma wins all purifying (objects) making utterence (of Mantras).

### Anuvaka LXII

1\. The truth is the supreme, the supreme is the truth; by the truth they do not fall from the heavenly world at any time; the truth is related to the sages, therefore, they enjoy in the truth.

2\. The austerity: there is no better austerity than the fast; which is the great austerity, that is not worth tolerating, that is non-injurable; therefore, they enjoy in the musterity.

3\. The self-restraint: therefore, the Brahmacarins (the students of the sacred knowledge), as a rule enjoy in the selfrestraint. the forest.

4\. The place: therefore, the seers enjoy in the peace in

5\. All the beings praise the giving; there is nothing more difficult than the giving; therefore, they enjoy in the giving.

6\. The religion: by the religion all this has been held; there is nothing more difficult than the religion; therefore, they enjoy in the religion.

7\. Many people (believe in) generating; therefore, they are born in plenty; therefore, many people enjoy in the generating. 8\. One says the fire (to be great); therefore, the fires should be heaped.

9\. One says the Agnihotra (to be great); therefore, they enjoy in the Agnihotra.

10\. The sacrifice: because the gods went to the heavens by the sacrifices, therefore, they enjoy in the sacrifices.

11\. The learned ones (believe in) the praise by the mind; therefore, the learned ones enjoy in the praise by the mind.

[[P325]]

289

12\. Brahma (believes in) the renunciation; Brahma is the supreme, the supreme is Brahma; Brahma finalized all the lower austerities in the renunciation.

13\. He, who knows thus, this is the Upanisat (mystic knowledge for him).

### Anuvaka LXIII

1\. Aruni, the son of Prajapati and Suparna, went to his fabber Prajapati asking what that prosperous one says to be the supreme. He (Prajapati) said this to him:- sun

2\. The wind blows by the truth; by the truth the shines in the heaven; the truth is the firm-standing of the speech, everything is established in the truth; therefore, they say the truth to be the supreme.

3\. By the austerity the gods obtained their position of the gods; by the susterity, by the austerity we destroy our enemies; everything is established in the austerity; therefore, they say the austerity to be supreme.

4\. By the self-restraint the self-restraining ones destroy their sins; by the self-restraint the Brahmacarins (students of the sacred knowledge)went to the heaven; the selfrestraint is non-injurable among the men; everything is established in the self-restraint, therefore, they say the self-restraint to be the supreme.

5\. By the peace the peaceful ones conduct the propitiation; the seers obtained the he even by the peace; the peace is not to be injured among the men; everything is established in the peace; therefore, they say the pe ace to be the supreme.

[[P326]]

290

6\. The giving is the best Daksine (sacrificial fee); in the world all the people depend upon the giver; by the giving they destroyed the enemies; by the giving they haters become friends; everything is established in the giving; therefore, they say the giving to be the supreme.

7\. The religion is the firm-standing of all the world, the subjects go to the religious one; by the religion he destroys the sins; everything is established in the religion; therefore, they say the religion to be the supreme.

8\. The generating is the firm-standing in the world; one spreading the line of the progeny well, becomes debtless among the fathers; that is the debtlessness of that; therefore, they say the generating to be the supreme.

9\. The fires are, verily, the three knowledges and the Devayane way (leading to the gods); the household fire is the Re, earth and Rathantara Saman; the Anvshary apac an a fire is the Yajus, the mid-region and Vamade vya Saman; the Ahavaniya fire is the Saman and the heavenly world and the Brhat Saman; therefore, they say the fire to be the supreme.

10\. The Agnihotra-sacrifice of the morning and the evening is the price of the house; well sacrificing and the well offering is the beginning of the sacrificial powers and the light of the heavenly world; therefore, they say the Agnihotra to be the supreme. 11\. The sacrifice: by the sacrifice the gods went to the heaven; by the sacrifice they destroyed the Asuras; by the sacrifice the haters become the friends; everything is established in the sacrifice; therefore, they say the sacrifice to be supreme.

[[P327]]

291

12\. The praise by the mind is pure and related to

Prajapati; by the praise of the mind one sees the good in his mind; the sages created the beings related to the mind; everything is established in the mind; therefore, they say the mind to be the supreme.

13\. The thinkers say Brahma as (to be) the renunétation. 14\. Brahma is all, most comfortful, self-born, Prajapati and the year.

15\. The year is that sun; who is this Purusa (Person) in the sun, that is Paramesthi Brahma, Atman (Self).

16\. By what rays the sun shines, by those Parjanya rains; by Parjanya the plants and the vegetables are born; by the plants and the vegetables comes the food, by the food come the breaths, by the breaths the vigour comes, by the vigour comes the austerity, by the austerity comes the faith; by the faith comes the intellect, by the intellect comes the thinking, by the thinking comes the mind, by the mind comes the peace, by the peace comes the heart, by the he art comes the memory, by the memory comes knowledge, by knowledge knows the self; therefore, by giving the food one gives all these; by the food come the breaths of the beings, by the breaths comes the mind, by the mind comes knowledge and by knowledge comes the bliss, Brahman, the cause of the world.

17\. That, verily, this Purusa (Person) is fivefold (made of five matters), of five selves, by whom all this earth, midregion, he even, quarters and sub-quarters have been pervaded; he is, verily, all this world; he is the born one and he is to be born; he is known by the desire of knowing, he is born of the settled order, most wealthy, the faith, the truth, mighty and above the darkness.

### Anuvaka LXIX

1\. of his sacrifice, who knows thus, Atman is the sacrificer, the faith is the wife, the body is the fuel, the chest is the altar, the little hairs of the body are Barhi-grass, the Veda is the flame, he arti is the stake, the desire is the ghee, the anger is the animal, the austerity is the fire, peace-making is the Dalceine (sacrificial fee), the speech is the Hotr-priest, the breath is the Udgatr-priest; the eye is the Adhvaryu-priest, the mind is the Brahma-priest, the ear is the Agnidhra-priest.

2\. Holding-time without the meal is the consecration; whath he eats that is the oblation; what he drinks that is his Somadrinking; what he enjoys that is the Upas ad a; whatever he moves,

[[P329]]

293

sits or stands, that is the Pravargya; what is the face that is the Ahavaniya fire; what is the Uttering that is the offering; what is his knowledge that is his sacrificing; whatever he eats at the evening and the morning, that is the fuel, what are the morning, the moon and bbe evening they are that Savanas (pressings of Soma).

3\. What are the days and the nights, they are the Darsa and the Purnamasa sacrifices; what are the half-months and the months, they are the Caturmasy as, what are the seasons they are the Pasubandhas, what are the years end the cycles of the years, the groups of the days they are bhe Serva-Vedas; verily, this is the Satra (session of the sacrifice, what is the death that the Avabhrthe (the bathing after the sacrifice).

4\. This is his Agnihotra-Satra (session of that sacrifice) up to his old age and the death. He, who knowing thus, dies in the Uttareyans (the period of the Sun's progress to the north), goes to the accompanying of the sun after going to the greatness of the goda. And he, who dies in the Dakaireyene (the period of the sun's progress to the south), goes to accompanying of the moon after going to the greatness of the fathers. The learned Brahmana wins these greatnesses of the sun and the moon, hence he obtains the greatness of Brahman, (yea) hence the greatness of Brahman. the Upanisat(mystic knowledge).
